# DHIS2 API Compatibility Matrix — DER 2.41–2.44

_Generated 2026-11-17 against DER `main` (apps/analytics modified)._

## Delivery log

Incremental wave execution against the baseline below. Each entry shifts a set of endpoints from `STUB-EMPTY`/`MISSING` to `NATIVE`. The full register further down is point-in-time; the per-batch deltas are listed here until the register is regenerated in bulk.

- **2026-11-17 — Wave 1 Batch 2** (18 endpoints — identity + account management). Commit: pending.
  - `GET /api/account/username`, `GET /api/account/password` (policy), `POST /api/account/password` (change — verifies old pw, writes new hash via `EdgeUserRepository.upsert`), `POST /api/account/validatePassword`, `POST /api/account/validateUsername`, `GET /api/account/linkedAccounts` (empty by design — DER binds to one DHIS2 tenant), `GET /api/account/listSessions` (live rows from `der.edge_session`), `POST /api/account` (403 — self-registration disabled on edge), `POST /api/account/recovery`, `POST /api/account/restore`, `POST /api/account/sendEmailVerification`, `GET /api/account/verifyEmail` — new `AccountController`.
  - `POST /api/auth/login` (credential verify, directs client to `/edge-api/v1/auth/sign-in` for session), `POST /api/auth/updatePassword`, `POST /api/auth/invite` (ACCEPTED-no-central), `POST /api/auth/registration` (403), `POST /api/auth/forgotPassword` (ACCEPTED-no-central), `POST /api/auth/passwordReset` (ACCEPTED-no-central) — new `Dhis2AuthController`.
  - `SecurityConfig` adds `permitAll` for the four pre-auth POSTs (`/api/auth/{login,forgotPassword,passwordReset,registration}`) so unauthenticated clients can reach the controller; the controller itself still verifies credentials where appropriate.
  - Posture: endpoints that require outbound SMTP or self-registration (recovery, restore, sendEmailVerification, invite, forgotPassword, passwordReset) return 200 `{status: ACCEPTED, central: false, message: ...}` so apps probing them at boot don't crash; registration + self-create return 403 so an open appliance cannot spawn accounts.
- **2026-11-17 — Wave 1 Batch 1** (15 endpoints — reference data + simple persistence). Commit: pending.
  - `GET /api/locales/countries`, `GET /api/locales/languages`, `GET /api/locales/ui`, `GET /api/locales/dbLocales`, `GET /api/locales/dbLocales/{uid}`, `POST /api/locales/dbLocales`, `DELETE /api/locales/dbLocales`, `DELETE /api/locales/dbLocales/{uid}` — new `LocalesController`, ISO 639/3166 from the JDK, dbLocales persisted under `der.system_setting` namespace `dbLocale:*`.
  - `GET /api/periodTypes` (seeded earlier in session), `GET /api/periodTypes/{name}`, `GET /api/periodTypes/relativePeriodTypes`, `PUT /api/periodTypes`, `PUT /api/periodTypes/{name}`, `PUT /api/periodTypes/{name}/translations` — added to `AppShellEmptySurfacesController`. Period types are a DHIS2 compile-time enum, so writes are no-ops matching central's silent-accept behaviour.
  - `GET /api/staticContent/{key}`, `POST /api/staticContent/{key}` — general-key handlers in `AppShellStubsController` backed by `der.system_setting` under `staticContent:*`. Keeps the pre-existing `logo_banner`/`logo_front` specific handlers unchanged.
  - `POST /api/systemSettings` (bulk upsert), `DELETE /api/systemSettings` (bulk clear) — added to `SystemSettingsController`.


## Source baseline

Union of four DHIS2 OpenAPI 3.0 specs, pulled from DHIS2's `/api/openapi/openapi.json` endpoint on the public im.dhis2.org play cluster:

| Version | Source instance | `/api/openapi/openapi.json` URL | Operations |
|---------|-----------------|--------------------------------|------------|
| 2.41    | `stable-2-41-11` | `https://play.im.dhis2.org/stable-2-41-11/api/openapi/openapi.json` | 2867 |
| 2.42    | `stable-2-42-7-1` | `https://play.im.dhis2.org/stable-2-42-7-1/api/openapi/openapi.json` | 2575 |
| 2.43    | `stable-2-43-2-1` | `https://play.im.dhis2.org/stable-2-43-2-1/api/openapi/openapi.json` | 2575 |
| 2.44    | `dev` (2.44-SNAPSHOT, `a6f555d`, built 2026-11-17) | `https://play.im.dhis2.org/dev/api/openapi/openapi.json` | 2592 |

**Fallback notes.** The spec's suggested docs.dhis2.org URLs (`.../dhis-core-version-{241..244}/openapi/openapi.json`) all return HTTP 404 at the time of writing — DHIS2 docs no longer publish per-version OpenAPI JSON on docs.dhis2.org. The cluster-hosted endpoints above are the authoritative live source. For 2.44 the only live instance is the rolling `dev` (`2.44-SNAPSHOT`) — if a stable 2.44.0 play instance is published later, re-run the extraction against it.

Path normalisation: 2.41 serves the OpenAPI with `servers.url` ending in `/api`, so its paths start at `/` (e.g. `/me`). 2.42+ have `servers.url` at root and paths start with `/api`. All paths in this document are normalised to the `/api/...` form.

## Summary

- Total unique (method, path) tuples across 2.41–2.44: **3028**
- **NATIVE**: 100 (3.3%)
- **PROXY**: 426 (14.1%)
- **STUB-TAILORED**: 27 (0.9%)
- **STUB-EMPTY**: 1563 (51.6%)
- **MISSING**: 912 (30.1%)

DER status legend:

| Code | Meaning |
|------|---------|
| `NATIVE` | A DER Spring controller handles the exact path with real DER-backed logic (DHIS2 schema mirror, journal, submission tables, etc.). |
| `PROXY` | No DER handler; path is in `Dhis2ApiProxyController.DEFAULT_ALLOW` → forwarded to `der.dhis2.central.base-url` **iff** central is configured and proxy enabled, otherwise falls back to graceful-empty. |
| `STUB-TAILORED` | Hand-crafted empty/placeholder shape in `AppShellEmptySurfacesController` or `AppShellStubsController`. Returns enough structure for the stock app shell to boot without crashing. |
| `STUB-EMPTY` | No dedicated handler; caught by `Dhis2ApiProxyController.gracefulEmpty()` which returns an empty list + pager (`GET /collection`), a 404 envelope (`GET /collection/{uid}`), or a fake ImportSummary job-ack (writes). Many of these are safe for read-only idempotent GETs but will drop writes silently. |
| `MISSING` | `STUB-EMPTY` catch-all on a non-GET method inside P0–P2 scope — silent 200 for a write where the app expects a real ImportSummary or 404. Treat as a known gap. |

**Native handler count**: 100 exact-match handlers across 18 distinct DER controller classes.

## By priority tier

| Tier | Endpoints | NATIVE | PROXY | STUB-TAILORED | STUB-EMPTY | MISSING |
|------|-----------|--------|-------|----------------|------------|---------|
| P0 | 173 | 31 | 24 | 7 | 47 | 64 |
| P1 | 1574 | 68 | 402 | 9 | 454 | 641 |
| P2 | 333 | 0 | 0 | 10 | 116 | 207 |
| P3 | 904 | 1 | 0 | 1 | 902 | 0 |
| P4 | 44 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 44 | 0 |

Tier definitions:

- **P0 — boot-blocker.** The stock DHIS2 React app shell crashes or refuses to render if this returns the wrong shape or a 404. Classic examples: `/api/me`, `/api/schemas`, `/api/systemSettings`, `/api/periodTypes`, `/api/loginConfig`, `/api/ping`.
- **P1 — core feature.** App boots but a primary feature is broken. `/api/analytics` for Data Visualizer, `/api/geoFeatures` for Maps, `/api/tracker/events` for Capture, `/api/dashboards` for Dashboard, `/api/metadata` for every app.
- **P2 — secondary feature degrades.** Interpretations, sharing dialog, user lookup, favorites, audits.
- **P3 — admin-only.** Data Administration, Maintenance, Metadata Import/Export, Resource Tables, SQL Views admin.
- **P4 — central-only (EXCLUDED).** App Hub installation, cluster leader election, central metadata push — explicitly out of scope for DER because they require a central cluster by definition.

## By app (observed hard-coded /api/* refs)

App bundles ship under `~/.der/apps/<id>/<version>/`. The register below is derived from a conservative grep of each bundle for hard-coded `/api/<root>[/...]` string literals. Minified bundles that build URLs dynamically (string concat around a `baseUrl` + resource name) will under-count; treat these numbers as a floor, not a ceiling.

| App | Observed roots | Matching endpoints | NATIVE | PROXY | STUB-T | STUB-E | MISSING |

| `eregisters` | 6 | 40 | 3 | 15 | 2 | 14 | 6 |
| `maps` | 9 | 87 | 13 | 43 | 3 | 20 | 8 |

## Phasing — proposed implementation waves

Waves ordered by (tier × app-coverage × endpoint fan-out). Endpoint counts are the number of OpenAPI tuples the wave is responsible for; actual Spring handler count will be smaller because one native handler can cover many OpenAPI tuples (sibling methods, path variants).

- **Wave 1 — P0 shell parity** (2–3 weeks, ~118 endpoints). Replace `gracefulEmpty` for `/api/account/**`, `/api/auth/**`, `/api/configuration/**`, `/api/dimensions/**`, `/api/locales/**`, `/api/periodTypes/**`, `/api/schemas/**`, `/api/staticContent/**`, `/api/systemSettings` (write path). Outcome: every DHIS2 app boots to first render without destructure crashes, regardless of whether the backend has real content.
- **Wave 2 — P1 analytics + metadata read** (4–6 weeks, ~1369 endpoints after Wave 1). Native handlers for `/api/analytics` (aggregate + event + enrollments + outliers), `/api/geoFeatures`, `/api/metadata` (bulk export/import), `/api/dataValueSets`, plus full read of metadata collections (`/api/dataElements`, `/api/indicators`, `/api/organisationUnits`, `/api/dashboards`, `/api/visualizations`, `/api/maps`, `/api/dataSets`). Unblocks Data Visualizer, Maps, Dashboard offline queries. Current PROXY entries become NATIVE.
- **Wave 3 — P1 tracker + capture write-side** (2–3 weeks, ~137 endpoints). Full `/api/tracker/**` native parity (events, enrollments, trackedEntities, relationships), including POST import with real ImportSummary + journal entry. Switches today's `MISSING` writes under tracker to NATIVE.
- **Wave 4 — P2 secondary** (3–4 weeks, ~333 endpoints). Interpretations, messageConversations, sharing, userLookup, favorites, audits, minMaxValues, lockExceptions. Enables full Dashboard discussion flows and Data Entry min/max validation offline.
- **Wave 5 — P3 admin** (4 weeks, ~903 endpoints). Data Administration, Maintenance, Metadata Import/Export UI, Resource Tables, SQL Views admin, Job Configurations (where applicable to edge).
- **Excluded — P4 central-only**: `/api/appHub` (install from online Hub), `/api/leader` (cluster leader election), `/api/synchronization/metadataPull` (central metadata push). These require a central cluster by design; DER's architecture is explicitly edge-first.
